  You've cast a spell this turn.

Steps: Cast Lagrella for its mana cost.
  When Lagrella enters, it exiles Phantom Steed until Lagrella leaves the battlefield. Resolve any additional effects of Lagrella's ETB.
  When Phantom Steed leaves the battlefield, Faceless Butcher is returned from exile to the battlefield.
  When Faceless Butcher enters, it exiles Lagrella until Faceless Butcher leaves the battlefield. Resolve any additional effects of Faceless Butcher's ETB.
  When Lagrella leaves the battlefield, Phantom Steed is returned from exile to the battlefield.
  When Phantom Steed enters, it exiles Faceless Butcher until Phantom Steed leaves the battlefield. Resolve any additional effects of Phantom Steed's ETB.
  When Faceless Butcher leaves the battlefield, Lagrella is returned from exile to the battlefield.
  Repeat from step 2.
Notes: Due to differences in card templating between the various banisher effects (linked triggered abilities vs "until" clause), steps 3, 5, and 7 may return the creature to the battlefield during the resolution of the preceding step rather than creating a separate event. This should not affect the overall execution of the combo, only how many objects pass through the stack (and thus rounds of priority available per loop).

## Combo 3234-3293-3967-5412
Cards: Emeria Shepherd + World Shaper + Yahenni, Undying Partisan + Coastal Hornclaw
Color identity: GWUB | Bracket: 1+ (Exhibition) | Popularity: 0 | Commander: legal
Produces: Infinite self-mill; Infinite creature LTB; Infinite creature ETB; Infinite creature sacrifice triggers; Infinite death triggers; Infinite landfall triggers; Return all nonland permanents from your graveyard to the battlefield
Start: Emeria Shepherd (battlefield); World Shaper (battlefield); Yahenni, Undying Partisan (battlefield); Coastal Hornclaw (battlefield)
Prerequisites: You control a Plains or have a Plains in your graveyard.
Steps: Activate Coastal Hornclaw by sacrificing a land.
  Repeat step 1 until you control no lands.
  Activate Yahenni by sacrificing World Shaper.
  When World Shaper dies, it triggers, return all lands from your graveyard to the battlefield tapped.
  When the lands enter, Emeria Shepherd triggers that many times.
  Resolve an Emeria Shepherd trigger that was triggered by a Plains, returning World Shaper from your graveyard to the battlefield.
  Resolve all remaining Emeria Shepherd triggers.
  Resolve the Yahenni ability.
  Repeat
Notes: You can return all nonland permanents in your graveyard and library to the battlefield if you control there are two or more Plains between your graveyard, library and battlefield. To do this, have all triggers from Emeria Shepherd target desired permanents. Then, resolve the Plains triggers first to return those cards to the battlefield, causing all other triggers to fizzle on the stack.

To do this, have all triggers from Emeria Shepherd target desired permanents. Then, resolve the Plains triggers first to return those cards to the battlefield, causing all other triggers to fizzle on the stack.

  When Sin dies, it triggers twice.
  Resolve the first trigger, returning Sin from your graveyard to the battlefield with a -1/-1 counter on it. As Sin enters, remove the -1/-1 counter from the creature sacrificed in step 1, causing Sin to enter with a +1/+1 counter on it instead.
  Resolve the second Sin trigger, putting the counters it had on it when it was sacrificed onto a creature you control and failing to shuffle it into your library as it's no longer in your graveyard.
  Resolve the Shilgengar ability from step 4.
  Repeat.
